# Implement merged-report-v2 §(d) R-01..R-19 — generalize /sc:troubleshoot

## Archive disposition — 2026-09-21

Implementation merged in IronbellyOrg/IronClaude PR #231. The historical Done status and reflection fields above are retained as recorded, not as a new QA certification. The final reflection contract reports success while its linked merged report says **BLOCK DONE** over process/evidence issues. Both are retained under `reflect/post-wt/`; this archival cleanup does not resolve that discrepancy or claim a fresh validation pass.

The complete original directory is preserved locally at `/config/IronClaude-cleanup-20260921-1454/uncommitted-artifacts/.dev/tasks/to-do/TASK-RF-troubleshoot-generalize-20260919-015257/`. Git retains the task, research, discovery records, output reports, validation summaries, consolidated QA findings, pre-reflection report, and final reflection contract/report. Intermediate reviews, raw outputs, snapshots, and nested agent memories remain only in the backup. Historical `to-do/` paths below refer to the original layout; retained records now live under `done/`. This local backup is not distributed with the repository.

## Task Overview

The sysbox retrospective (three-model debugging session, 2026-09-18) showed that the current `/sc:troubleshoot` protocol has no execution-locus step (where the code ran vs where the symptom printed), no pre-hypothesis discriminating-experiment step, no anti-cosmetic-loop guard, and lets a calibrated-0.42 report headline a definite root cause. `merged-report-v2.md` §(d) is the adversarially-merged, spec-panel-critiqued refactor (transferability rescored 0.60 → 0.90) that a weak model can execute as a checklist. This task lands it in the IronClaude repo.

Concretely, in a git worktree at `.dev/worktrees/troubleshoot-generalize` on branch `feature/troubleshoot-generalize` cut from `origin/master`, this task: (1) creates four new refs under `src/superclaude/skills/sc-troubleshoot-protocol/refs/` (`agent-assertions.md`, `primitive-differential.md`, `environment-deltas.md`, `probe-packs/read-parse.md`); (2) edits `SKILL.md` at ~49 seams bottom-up, 7 substantive refs, 5 rename-only hardening refs (HC0-HC5 rename), the `confidence-calibrator` and `evidence-validator` agents, and `commands/troubleshoot.md`; (3) adds one markdownlint exclude line to `.pre-commit-config.yaml`; (4) builds the LLM-free pytest harness (`tests/troubleshoot/_assertions.py`, `_procedures.py`), 21 spec-named test files, ~125 fixture files including a sha256 `MANIFEST` for the vendored sysbox regression set, and updates the two existing tests that R-16/R-17 break. Source of truth is `src/superclaude/`; `.claude/` is `make sync-dev` output and is NEVER staged. Every markdown edit anchors on quoted `old_string` text, never on line numbers, and follows the G-07 ordering ledger in `07-gap-fill-reconcile.md`.

Quality: three M3 lens-based QA gates (markdown build; tests build; post-completion final state) at full intensity, one M4 source-fidelity gate partitioned R-01..R-09 / R-10..R-19, a dedicated-subagent POST reflect runner (penultimate item), and a conventional commit on the feature branch.

## Key Objectives

The following objectives MUST be achieved by this task:

1. **Worktree + baseline:** `feature/troubleshoot-generalize` worktree from `origin/master` under `.dev/worktrees/`; baseline `make verify-sync` (`✅ All components in sync.`) and pytest (`70 passed, 1 failed` known-red `backtest/test_backtest_e4.py`) recorded BEFORE any edit; `.pre-commit-config.yaml` markdownlint exclude alternative `tests/troubleshoot/fixtures/.*` added first.
2. **Four new refs + Refs-table rows** (`agent-assertions.md` canonical 19-row assertion table per reconcile §X-3; `primitive-differential.md` R-05 six-kind table + bracketing; `environment-deltas.md` D9 body; `probe-packs/read-parse.md` v2:336-344) registered in the SKILL `## Refs` table (D10) before any wave text cites them.
3. **SKILL.md** edited at every G-07 seam, bottom-up (Wave 5 → 4.5 → 3 → 1.7 → 1.6 → 1 → Output Contract → wave map), including the D4-fixed S1.6.4 discriminator trigger placed BEFORE the verdict branch, the three spawn-site kwargs (§X-4), the `status: blocked` enum (D2), `contract_version 1.2.0` (D3), Will-Not bullets + `cosmetic_overrun` footer (D7), `split_pending` (D8), and the HC0-HC5 rename (R-17) applied AFTER the R-16 enum edit.
4. **Refs** — R-16 five-token enum (`blocked-on-authorization` appended at the END) BEFORE the R-17 rename across `hardening-output-contract.md`, `pipeline-hardening-closure.md`, `report-template.md`, `unmask-and-sweep.md`, `runtime-entrypoint-verification.md`, `contract-enumeration.md`, `effective-input-proof.md`; D11 guard prints `GUARD OK`; substantive edits to `diagnosability-audit.md`, `report-template.md`, `hypothesis-card-template.md`, `triage-checklist.md`, `escalation-rubric.md`.
5. **Agents + command** — CAL Inputs/step 5b/Stage-2 row/`split_pending`/Notes and VAL Inputs (incl. `locus_path`, `output_dir`)/2b cite line/`## Structural assertions` table/status `blocked` (R-14, §X-4, §X-5, D2, D12); `commands/troubleshoot.md` `:69` + `:103` (R-18).
6. **Validation** — `make sync-dev && make verify-sync` GREEN in the worktree plus the scoped `diff -rq`/`diff -q` check; `pre-commit run markdownlint --files <19 md files>` exit 0 with no rewrite; HC guard 0 lines; `make lint` clean; `git status --porcelain | grep '^A.*\.claude/'` empty.
7. **Tests** — `_assertions.py` (19-entry `FLAGS`, evaluators, loader) + `_procedures.py` (P1-P25) + fixtures per harness §4/§5 + 21 test files with the depth-3 function names (Phase 7) + updates to `test_hardening_h1.py:46` and `test_hardening_verdict.py:51` (landed in Phase 3, items 3.4 / 3.13, beside the edits that break them); `uv run pytest tests/troubleshoot/ -v` green except the known-red e4; full `uv run pytest -q` green vs baseline.
8. **Quality gates** — M3 markdown gate (10 agents: 5 rf-qa + 5 rf-qa-qualitative, N=15) after Phase 5; M3 tests gate (10 agents: 5 rf-qa + 5 rf-qa-qualitative, N=10; reviewed set ≥ 1500 lines) + M4 fidelity gate (2 partitions × 2 agents + 1 cross-source agent) after Phase 8; post-completion M3 gate (12 agents: 6 rf-qa + 6 rf-qa-qualitative, all 8 standard lenses; reviewed set > 3000 lines) on the final state before the commit; POST reflect runner (deep) penultimate; conventional commit `feat(troubleshoot): …` on the feature branch, never touching `.claude/`.

### Handoff File Convention

This task uses intra-task handoff patterns. Items write intermediate outputs to:
**`/config/workspace/IronClaude/.dev/tasks/to-do/TASK-RF-troubleshoot-generalize-20260919-015257/phase-outputs/`**

Subdirectories:
- `discovery/` - naming ledger, insertion-anchor inventory, preserve-baseline, baseline captures
- `test-results/` - raw command output + summaries (sync-dev, verify-sync, markdownlint, guard, ruff, pytest)
- `reviews/` - quality review verdicts
- `plans/` - conditional-action outputs (verdicts / fix plans)
- `reports/` - aggregated reports, QA input manifests

QA reports are written to `${TASK_DIR}qa/exec-md/` (Phase 6 gate), `${TASK_DIR}qa/exec-tests/` (Phase 9 M3 gate), `${TASK_DIR}qa/exec-fidelity/` (Phase 9 M4 gate), `${TASK_DIR}qa/exec-phase10/` (Phase 10 post-completion gate). Reflect POST artifacts go to `${TASK_DIR}reflect/post/`.

These files persist across all batches and session rollovers. Later items read them by path.

**Path key used in every item below (absolute values):** `${TASK_DIR}` = `/config/workspace/IronClaude/.dev/tasks/to-do/TASK-RF-troubleshoot-generalize-20260919-015257/`; `${RES}` = `${TASK_DIR}research/`; `${WT}` = `/config/workspace/IronClaude/.dev/worktrees/troubleshoot-generalize/`; `${SKILL}` = `${WT}src/superclaude/skills/sc-troubleshoot-protocol/SKILL.md`; `${REFS}` = `${WT}src/superclaude/skills/sc-troubleshoot-protocol/refs/`; `${CAL}` = `${WT}src/superclaude/agents/confidence-calibrator.md`; `${VAL}` = `${WT}src/superclaude/agents/evidence-validator.md`; `${CMD}` = `${WT}src/superclaude/commands/troubleshoot.md`; `${TESTS}` = `${WT}tests/troubleshoot/`; `${FIX}` = `${WT}tests/troubleshoot/fixtures/`; `${V2}` = `/config/workspace/Coder/.claude/worktrees/gh-automation-orca-run/.dev/research/sysbox-retrospective-20260918/merged-report-v2.md`.

Anchor = step-1 fallback bullet ending `note the fallback in the audit log.` (unique). Wave 1 steps use `N. **Verb** — prose` with 3-space sub-bullets (`${RES}02-patterns-conventions.md` §1.3).
  - **Action**: ONE Edit on `${SKILL}` (per consolidated finding F-I4 — the `new_string` is fixed, not composed at execution time): `old_string` = `note the fallback in the audit log.` → `new_string` = `note the fallback in the audit log.` + newline + the following block VERBATIM (1b. line at column 0; sub-bullets 3-space-indented `- `; the Rules sub-list 5-space-indented; composed from 03 §R-01 v2:216-228 with spec ids replaced per 03 I-19 — verbatim except the v2:225 ten-item prompt list, which D9 folds into the eight-delta `refs/environment-deltas.md` table — plus the R-05 pack-load rule (v2:314), the RQ-06 pasted-log rule, and the v2:229 Must-NOT closing sentence):

````markdown
1b. **Write the execution-locus card** — `<output-dir>/execution-locus.md`, on every invocation including pasted-log issues, re-derived each run and never carried over. One line each; `unknown` is legal, blank is illegal:
   - `PRINT-SITE:` the file / stream / job / terminal where the failing line appeared. Copy verbatim from the issue.
   - `RUN-SITE:` the `file:line` from step 1 that emits that line, `@` the process/host/environment label under which it executes. Initial value `pending-producers` if step 1 found none; overwritten by S1.6.0b step 4 with the surviving producer's `file:line`. Validator A7 rejects the sentinel at finalize **only when `producers.md` has ≥1 producer row**; otherwise the sentinel is legal with `status: partial`.
   - `SAME-ENV:` `yes` / `no` / `unknown`. Derivation: `no` if the issue text or the step-1 grounding (CI matrix, workflow file) names more than one machine, process, job, or environment label; `yes` if exactly one is named and RUN-SITE is in it; otherwise `unknown`.
   - `OBSERVE-VIA:` `same-shell` / `remote-command` / `artifact-file` / `nobody` — how RUN-SITE's stdout and exit status can be read from here.
   - If the issue names more than one arm (matrix, "works in X, fails in Y"): repeat the four lines per arm, then per *passing* arm add `CONTROL-PROOF:` `yes: <file:line>` / `no: <file:line>` / `unknown` — the line where that arm executes the RUN-SITE producer.
   - Rules:
     - `SAME-ENV ≠ yes` ⇒ every probe result and every hypothesis card carries `runs-in=<RUN-SITE label>`; a card without it is returned unread (exactly like a card without `consistency_with_docs`, Wave 1.7 step 1). `runs-in=unknown` is legal but the calibrator caps Runtime check ≤ 0.5 with Notes `locus_unknown` (C3b, refs/agent-assertions.md).
     - `SAME-ENV ≠ yes` ⇒ read the optional ref `refs/environment-deltas.md` (a generic per-delta prompt table); deltas the model cannot prove identical are candidate discriminator rows for S1.6.4 and Wave 1.7. Loading is locus-triggered, never `--type`-triggered. Absent ref ⇒ audit line `optional_ref_absent: refs/environment-deltas.md`, no Grounding Gap.
     - Step 2 executes any repro through `OBSERVE-VIA`; `nobody` ⇒ record "no repro available: run-site unobservable" and continue.
     - **Verdict source**: when `OBSERVE-VIA = artifact-file` or the failing arm is a CI job, fetch the failing arm's job log to `<output-dir>/job-<id>.log` *before* reading any verdict; the verdict is the last line in that log matching the harness's result marker, never the workflow- or job-level conclusion; no marker line ⇒ `verdict: unobservable`, treated as FAIL. Worked example only (not a mandatory tool): `gh run view <run-id> --json jobs --jq '.jobs[]|[.databaseId,.name,.conclusion]'` then `gh api repos/<owner>/<repo>/actions/jobs/<id>/logs > <output-dir>/job-<id>.log`; marker `^RESULT:` (a `--log-failed` view is empty under continue-on-error). Validator A8 checks the log exists at finalize.
     - A passing arm may be cited as a control only with `CONTROL-PROOF: yes: <file:line>`; otherwise Symptom coverage ≤ 0.5, Notes `control_unproven` (calibrator C3).
     - Probe packs: if `refs/probe-packs/<kind>.md` exists for any primitive kind listed for a surviving producer, load it at S1.6.4 — the load set is kind-based, never runtime- or `--type`-based.
     - Pasted-log or single-process unit-test issues: the four lines are still written (`SAME-ENV: yes`, `OBSERVE-VIA: same-shell`), no optional ref is loaded.
   - Never block on `unknown`, ask the user, add a flag or an approval step, mandate an environment-specific cell, or duplicate the producer/transformer/consumer schema of refs/runtime-entrypoint-verification.md.
````

  - **Output**: step 1b present between step 1's fallback bullet and step 2.

  - **Completion gate**: checks hold. J1 clause. Once done, mark this item as complete.

### Phase 9: Phase Gate — M3 lens-based QA on the test build (5 rf-qa + 5 rf-qa-qualitative = 10 agents, N = 10) then M4 source-document fidelity gate (2 partitions × 2 agents + 1 cross-source agent)

Gate sizing (I19/I22 full): the reviewed set (`${TASK_DIR}phase-outputs/reports/qa-input-manifest-tests.md`) is 24 Python files + 129 fixtures; the changed-line count is ≥ 1500 (the harness §4 fixture bodies alone are 1558 lines (`${H}` :307-1896 + :1897-2214), plus the two harness modules and ~153 test cases across 21 files) ⇒ tier 1500-3000 ⇒ 5 structural (template-conformance, internal-consistency, evidence-quality, completeness, hc-guard/lint domain) + 5 content (actionability, numbers-metrics, domain-accuracy, traceability domain, crossref-chain) = 10 agents (qualitative finding Q-I1); adversarial N = 10. Reports go to `${TASK_DIR}qa/exec-tests/`. M4 (I21 mandatory — every output derives from `${V2}` §(d)): source docs `${V2}` §(d) (~500 lines) + `${RES}07-gap-fill-reconcile.md` (358 lines) ⇒ partition R-01..R-09 / R-10..R-19 with 2 agents each (fidelity reports to `${TASK_DIR}qa/exec-fidelity/`); ordering AFTER the M3 gate. Serialized fix per I20; max 3 cycles per gate; regression → monotonicity → hard-cap precedence.

**[2026-09-19 build]** - Deviation from template B3 (single-paragraph items):
- **Expected:** every checklist item as ONE paragraph.
- **Actual:** items use the bold-header + Context / Action / Output / Verification / Completion-gate sub-bullet form.
- **Rationale:** the BUILD_REQUEST explicitly required this structured form (`Verify:` prefix, evidence-absence markers) and it matches the task-builder SKILL's Phase-item template; each item remains a self-contained prompt with all six B2 elements.

**[2026-09-19 build]** - Deviation from the G-07 group numbering (not from its rules):
- **Expected:** reconcile edit groups 2 (enum) → 3 (rename) → 4 (new refs + rows + plumbing) → 5 (agents) → 6 (other refs) → 7 (SKILL waves) → 8 (command), each complete before the next.
- **Actual:** Phase 1 (new refs + Refs rows) → Phase 2 (SKILL: enum → rename → plumbing → waves bottom-up) → Phase 3 (refs: enum → rename → guard → plumbing → substantive) → Phase 4 (agents → command), per the BUILD_REQUEST phase order.
- **Rationale:** all six G-07 RULES hold (R-16 enum precedes R-17 rename within every shared file as separate items; R-01 text before R-02 in file order is irrelevant to text anchors and both land before R-04/R-10 consumers are gated; new ref + Refs row (Phase 1) precede every wave citation; SKILL waves are bottom-up; every Edit anchors on quoted text); grouping by FILE reduces context switching for the executor. G-07 group order (5 agents, 6 other refs BEFORE 7 SKILL waves): Phases 2-4 edit disjoint files; every SKILL citation of a ref section is by section NAME (not line), and the Refs-table rows (Phase 1) already exist, so the G-07 group order is satisfied at the file level once Phase 4 completes; the intra-phase order is bottom-up by anchor. Groups 5/6-before-7 is honoured for the only cross-file DEPENDENCY (Refs rows) and relaxed for citations-by-name (2.36 R-08 step 4.5 and 2.39 R-09 cite the HCT form that 3.30 lands) — cycle-2 F-A2.
